# Updated coverage: every dashboard stock

1,955 stocks / 4,078 annual entries. Historical review status is preserved. Passing the research screen is not a current BUY decision. Watch entries below have stored triage fields; calculated price/score features were refreshed for all 348 picks, not every watch entry.

Proposed screen: an existing CORE match (C2/C3/C5/C7), MEDIUM/HIGH review confidence and positive reported OPM. Normalised recurring margin and funding still require verification.
